# H09 evidence / Протокол H09

Review required; no automatic HW VERIFIED promotion.

## Reviewed result

The physical 40 V expansion completed CV40 successfully. At CC40, the stable
readings were PSU `MEAS:VOLT?=33.1471`, `MEAS:CURR?=0.0214744`; KEL103
`MEAS:VOLT?=33.148`, `MEAS:CURR?=0.0202`, `MEAS:POW?=0.6695`; DMM7510
`:READ?=33.152460`. Error-queue reads were `0, "No error"`. The operator saw
approximately 33 V but did not see current or confirm the CC indicator. The
runner stopped because its 37.8–40.0 V threshold was too narrow for the
manufacturer's stated Series current accuracy. This is a runner acceptance
criteria failure, not evidence of a library SCPI failure. Outputs and load were
turned off and the operator confirmed disconnected wiring. The corrected
acceptance criteria require a repeat before CC40 can pass. See
[H09 preflight and corrected criteria](../H09_PREFLIGHT.ru.md).
